# Repository Change Archive — File_66 August 5–8 Clarification and Recovery Closure

## Archive function

This non-controlling addendum records the File_66 work performed after the August 4 File_66 Finalization / full post-final pressure-test layer preserved in Repository Change Archive v1.46.

The v1.47 full replacement must preserve the complete v1.46 source **byte-for-byte as its suffix**. The companion assembler fails closed unless the supplied v1.46 parent has exactly the required SHA-256 and byte size.

## 1. August 5–7 post-final File_66 additions

The working File_66 advanced beyond its August 4 `B176` / 64-guard Finalization state through a sequence of bounded additions and focused checks. The August 7 parent source used for the final recovery ended at:

```text
Appendix B:             B1–B470; 470/470 PASS
Appendix G arithmetic:  B249–B470; 222/222 PASS
Machine Guards:         91 unique
```

The post-August-4 additions included the SP-215 / Jeremiah / Ramesside appendix work, Appendix G Mirror-closure work, the expanded symmetry / center field, Isaiah–Daniel integrations, and the Manasseh–Zechariah continuation through `B470`.

Those additions did not erase the August 4 full pressure-test baseline. They constitute bounded post-final amendments with their own focused or appendix-level verification states.

## 2. Center/midpoint terminology audit

The August 7 audit found no general arithmetic-average failure, but it identified an operator-language ambiguity: File_66 used `center`, `midpoint`, `axis`, and `average` for mathematically different objects.

The controlling distinctions are now:

| Object | File_66 term |
|---|---|
| Same-side coordinate with equal arms | fixed same-side center |
| Midpoint after no-year-zero linearization | civil-linear midpoint coordinate |
| Equal endpoint-label sums | complementary pair-sum |
| Cross-axis elapsed duration | civil span |
| Half of an elapsed span | half-span |
| Important non-equidistant coordinate | pivot |
| Arithmetic junction | hub |
| Middle interval | central segment |
| Common interval counted inside larger arms | shared overlap interval |
| Ordinary unit-bearing division | mean / average |

The critical correction is:

```text
(711 + 586)/2 = 648.5
(656 + 641)/2 = 648.5
```

These are legitimate same-side BC label averages only. They do **not** define the midpoint of the cross-axis `1296` civil spans.

The active no-year-zero construction is:

```text
711 + 586 = 656 + 641 = 1297
1297 − 1 = 1296
1296 = 648 + 648.
```

Accordingly:

- `1297` is the complementary pair-sum;
- `1296` is the civil cross-axis span;
- `648 + 648` is an equal half-span partition;
- the `648` values are durations, not year coordinates;
- `648.5 BC` is not registered.

The diagnostic directional civil midpoints `63 BC`, `8 BC`, `AD 8`, and `AD 63` remain omitted from the coordinate register.

## 3. Retention of the `693.5 BC` civil-linear midpoint

`693.5 BC` remains valid because it is independently obtained after continuous no-year-zero mapping:

```text
L(B BC) = 1 − B
L(AD D) = D.
```

The five admitted shells share the same civil-linear midpoint and exact equal half-spans:

```text
15   = 7.5    + 7.5
215  = 107.5  + 107.5
1075 = 537.5  + 537.5
1505 = 752.5  + 752.5
2795 = 1397.5 + 1397.5.
```

`693.5 BC` remains derived, non-historical, non-calendrical, and non-Primary.

## 4. Latent `656 BC` Manasseh mercy node

The amendment registers only `656 BC` as a generated A7 Verbatim/Schematic interior node. It does not date Manasseh's captivity, prayer, release, or restoration.

The local arithmetic is:

```text
696 − 656 = 40
656 − 641 = 15
711 − 656 = 55
656 − 586 = 70
15 + 40 + 15 = 70
55 + 15 + 55 = 125
15 + 40 + 15 + 55 = 125
70 + 70 − 15 = 125.
```

Second Chronicles 33:11–13 supplies the undated narrative class of seizure, humiliation, prayer, divine hearing, and restoration. The proposed `656 → 641 BC = 15` mercy tail remains strong structural / providential inference, not recovered event dating.

## 1. File_66 textual and chronological seed

File_66 opens with Hezekiah at age `25`, his first-year Temple purification, and the text's binding of the king's added life to the deliverance of Jerusalem. Its controlling local sign-field is:

```text
711 BC → 701 BC = 10
701 BC → 686 BC = 15
711 BC → 686 BC = 25.
```

The two whole-year biographical rails are:

```text
750 → 725 → 711 → 696 BC
740 → 715 → 701 → 686 BC,
```

with a ten-year translation at every corresponding node. The `701 BC` joint death horizon of Hezekiah and Jerusalem remains a strong theological-chronological thesis grounded in the textual promise to preserve both king and city.

## 2. Dual-epoch synchronism and paired signs of Ahaz

The Hezekiah–Ahaz co-regency and sole-reign epochs generate the four-corner matrix:

```text
725 / 715 / 711 / 701 BC
```

with horizontal `10` and vertical `14`. The historically executed diagonal preserves the Samaria siege and the `701 BC` Assyrian crisis; the latent diagonal remains counterfactual and may not replace the historical event dates.

The Immanuel sign and Dial-of-Ahaz sign are coordinated by their theological substance:

```text
Ahaz / house of David + Jerusalem spared
↔
Hezekiah + Jerusalem spared.
```

The child-sign `10/11` chronology is subordinate to this king-and-city preservation correspondence.

## 3. A7, the fifth expansion, and the Abrahamic field

A7, Athaliah's seventh-year boundary, remains primary. A6 is the six-completed-year audit. The complete package is:

```text
A7 surplus = 50
966 BC → 1016 BC = 50
1446 → 1016 → 586 = 430 + 430
10/50 = 1/5.
```

The forward city arm is:

```text
701 − 586 = 115 = 5 × 23
115 × 25/23 = 125
125 − 115 = 10
711 − 586 = 125.
```

Hezekiah's death divides the `125`:

```text
711 → 686 = 25
686 → 586 = 100
25 + 100 = 125
1/5 + 4/5 = 5/5.
```

The raw and Key-completed composites remain distinct:

```text
50 + 115 = 165 = 11 × 15
50 + 125 = 175.
```

## 4. `15/150/1500`, exile, and restoration

The decimal ladder is:

```text
15
150 = 10 × 15
1500 = 100 × 15.
```

The translated `25`-year pairs are:

```text
711 → 686 = 25
561 → 536 = 25
711 − 561 = 686 − 536 = 150.
```

The complete A7 restoration field is:

```text
886 → 711 → 536 = 175 + 175 = 350
606 → 536 = 70 = 1/5 of 350
711 → 606 = 105 = 7 × 15
606 → 536 = 70 = 7 × 10.
```

The preferred Jehoiachin-release state is `561 BC`; the lesser `562 BC` option is acknowledged without leading.

## 5. File_19 residue and the `185150` companion

The reciprocal File_19 update makes explicit:

```text
184900 = 430²
185000 = 184900 + 100
801 → 701 → 686 → 586 = 100 + 15 + 100 = 215.
```

File_66 supplies the companion:

```text
184900 → 185000 → 185150
          100       150
100 + 150 = 250 = 10 × 25.
```

The complete A7 outer envelope is:

```text
184800 → 184900 → 185000 → 185150
          100       100       150
100 + 100 + 150 = 350.
```

The charted Ahaziah relation is:

```text
886 − 841 = 45
841 − 836 = 5
886 − 836 = 50.
```

At `841 BC` the tenfold Priestly form is:

```text
1991 − 841 = 1150 = 50 × 23
2091 − 841 = 1250 = 50 × 25
1150 × 25/23 = 1250.
```

## 6. Precession Square and Isaiah 37 cosmology

The `430/432` pair shares `2580` precessional days under the alternate and standard Great-Year values. The rare square state remains:

```text
25921 = 161² = (7 × 23)² = 23² × 49.
```

Its Priestly root is:

```text
161 × 25/23 = 175.
```

The celestial capstone is:

```text
185150 × 7 = 1296050 = 50 × 25921
50 × 25920 = 1296000
1296050 − 1296000 = 50.
```

This is a scale translation of the count `50`: the human royal gap is represented cosmologically through fifty Great-Year cycles, whose one-year-per-cycle `25921/25920` difference accumulates to `50`. The units do not collapse.

Isaiah 37 supplies the literary sequence of failed national gods, YHWH of Hosts, Daughter Zion, self-grown produce, renewed agriculture, remnant, Davidic preservation, one angel, `185000`, Nisroch, and Ararat. The shared `סָפִיחַ` vocabulary with Leviticus 25 creates a strong Sabbath-year resonance; a formal Jubilee observance is not asserted.

## 7. The modulus-`11` result and terminal ledger

Under A7, the declared eighteen-value whole-year ledger yields:

```text
n = 18
total = 394
contiguous spans = 171
multiples-of-11 spans = 29.
```

Prefix residue `9` appears seven times and generates `21` of the `29` hits. The accepted conditional permutation tail is approximately:

```text
p ≈ 0.00612.
```

A6 yields `18` hits and no effect. The Northern ledger yields `8` hits, with modulus `11` ranking last among `2–25`; the control eliminates a generic-regnal-ledger confound but is not itself a significant positive result.

The terminal whole-year and month-resolution states coexist:

```text
Manasseh 55 + post-Manasseh 55 = 110
Amon through Zedekiah = 666 months
666 / 6 kings = 111 months
919 → 586 = 333 years = 3 × 111 years.
```

The `333`-year and `666`-month states retain their unit firewall.

## 8. Joshua reversal and closure rails

Joshua's `110`-year life is compared with the terminal Verbatim/Schematic royal `110`:

```text
1476 → 1366 = 110
696 → 586 = 110.
```

The theological direction is reversed: Joshua leads Israel into the land; the terminal royal body ends with expulsion from it.

The closure rails are:

```text
1446 → 1016 → 586 = 430 + 430
1366 → 976 → 586 = 390 + 390.
```

`976 BC` remains schematic and does not replace the historical Temple state.

## Source-history correction ledger routed from Final File_65

## Appendix E. Source-history correction ledger

This appendix preserves discussion corrections so later readers do not inherit superseded reasoning. It is non-proof-bearing and remains a candidate for routing to the Repository Change Archive at Finalization.

| Item | Correction |
|---|---|
| “Regular MT produces no `1290` event” | Incorrect. Regular MT supplies the central `1876 BC → 586 BC = 1290` through Jacob's Entry and the Triple-`430`. Only the two flanking columns depend on localized LXX `−33`. |
| A6 and A7 as coequal Judah-ledger choices | Superseded. A7, the seventh-year boundary, leads the verbatim / schematic ledger and yields `50`. A6 is the subordinate six-completed-year audit yielding `49`. |
| Athaliah `7` as a printed completed reign duration | Incorrect terminology. The text gives six years of concealment while she was reigning and action in the seventh year. A7 is boundary inclusion. |
| A6 as an objection to A7 | Incorrect. A6 exposes the compensating `292 + 6 + 1 = 299` seam; `300/299` restores the boundary year without changing the source durations. |
| `886 BC` left unsuffixed | Superseded by author direction and phase execution. Use local schematic `886n BC`; `885n BC` is the hidden `299` head. |
| Terminal half-year as loose rounding | Replaced by `(11 + 0.25) + (11 + 0.25) = 22.5`; the half-year resolves `586t → 586n` outside `299/300`. |
| `50 years + 180/190 days` as phase-resolved net surplus | State conflation. The primary A7 phase-resolved surplus is `50`, with Chronicles `+10` days; `180/190` is the source-retained calendar payload. |
| Tishri 1 and Tishri 10 as two explicit Jubilee proclamations | Too strong. Tishri 1 is the civil-year trumpet marker; Tishri 10 is the explicit Jubilee-proclamation marker. |
| File_55 thirds relation as scribal proof | Overstated. `1446 → 886n → 606 = 560 + 280` is exact after independent generation of `886n`; rank as cross-file corroboration / providential synchronization. |
| `16 × 70` as independent proof | Over-count. It is the closure of `130 + 430 + 430 + 130`, important but entailed once the frame is admitted. |
| Jehoiachin release left dependency-blocked at `561/560` | Refined. Adopt `561 BC` as preferred local historical comparison; retain `562/561 BC` accession adjacency and `562 BC` audit alternative. |
| Ezekiel apparatus “recommends” the twelfth year | Incorrect. BHS prints the twelfth year and records an eleventh-year alternative in a few Hebrew manuscripts, Lucianic Greek, and Syriac. |
| LXX Leviticus / Luke lexical inventory pending | Verified. LXX Lev 25 distinguishes `λύτρωσις` / cognates from `ἄφεσις`; Luke 2:38 and 4:18 preserve the pair. |
| Luke Jubilee scholarship as consensus | Overstated. It is substantial and representative but debated. |
| `5249 BC` as merely implied | Superseded. Register locally as `5249n BC = 5256n − 7`; no global promotion before Finalization. |
| `AD 30 → AD 38 = 7` | Arithmetic error. The seven-year band is `AD 30 → AD 37`. |
| `6 BC + 36 = AD 30` | No-year-zero error. The full `36`-year span ends at `AD 31`; the `72`-year rail lands at `725 BC`. |
| `AD 34n/34t` treated as executed | Not supported yet. Retain as appendix-only author-directed shadow until the full phase derivation is stated. |
| Siege opening at bare `589 BC` | Under-specified. Use `589t/588n BC`; File_17's `588 BC` remains the Nisan/default shorthand. |
| Attribution of `430.5` | The author and parent files already control the `Temple 430.5 month-state bridge`; only the Ezekiel-as-round-rendering interpretation is local. |
| `430 − 394.5 = 36.5` | Incorrect. `430.5 − 394.5 = 36`, decomposing textually as `4 + 36 = 40`. |
| `430` reckoned `970n → 586t` | Incorrect as a span. The `430.5` is a sum of printed reigns equal to the schematic field at another resolution, not an ordinary accession-to-fall subtraction. |
| “Drop the `3.5`” | Over-broad. `3.5 years + 1 month = 43 months = 1290 days` is exact in its own calendar state. |
| `4600` as the twenty-third-year deportation | Incorrect. `4600 = 3023 + 832 + 745`; the twenty-third-year figure is `745`. |
| 2 Chronicles ends with Jehoiachin | Incorrect. Chronicles ends with Cyrus's decree. Kings and Jeremiah end with Jehoiachin at the king's table. |
| `1876 − 1335 = 561` | Incorrect. `1876 − 1335 = 541`; the `561` instance runs from `1896`, and the `536` instance from `1871`. |
| `587t` giving `49` | Reversed. Under A7, `587t` gives `51`; `49` comes from the adjacent `967n` Temple state. |
| Famine translated seven-year bands as independent witnesses | Over-count. Uniform subtraction preserves width automatically. |
| Multiple `45`s as separate evidence | Over-count. They express the shared `1335 − 1290 = 45` differential. |
| Hebrew Daniel 9 Flood term as Noahic lexical identity | Incorrect. The Greek has the lexical correspondence; Hebrew is typological only. |

## 1. Amendment classification and protected baseline

The July 27 work was classified as a substantive High-risk post-final amendment because it added:

- a rewritten and expanded §8;
- new Jacob, Cainan, Terah, Daniel, Flood, and dual-monarchy states;
- new arithmetic matrices and theological synthesis;
- new dependencies and Machine Guards;
- a new Appendix D;
- an enlarged Appendix C verification surface;
- a materially expanded final theorem.

The original File_64 Judges proof spine remained protected:

```text
Jephthah 300/299
+ post-Jephthah 91
+ complete Judges 111 + 299 = 410
+ 430/450/490 opening–joint–closure
= lesser 1016 BC Judges–Sanctuary Nexus.
```

The eighteen-row Judges corpus, `111 + 299 = 410`, direct `430`, explicit `450`, Joshua-extended `490`, three-position `1016 BC` convergence, First Samuel recapitulation, and Judges 17–21 negative-sanctuary closure were not replaced.

The full pressure test later confirmed `67/67` protected baseline subsections exact.

## 2. Anchored schematic-ledger principle

The amendment clarified that schematic chronology is generated from textually or historically controlled events rather than from free-floating numbers.

| Field | Historical / textual anchor | Schematic function |
|---|---|---|
| Earlier biblical chronology | primary `1446 BC` Exodus state | regular chronology extends backward through the patriarchs toward Adam |
| Judges | `1446/1406 BC` Exodus / Conquest field | bounded Judges durations extend forward to `1016/996/956 BC` |
| Kings of Israel | historical Fall of Samaria at `722t/721n BC` | reign ledger projects backward to the lesser `963t/n BC` head |
| Kings of Judah | historical Fall of Jerusalem at `587/586 BC` | sanctuary ledger projects backward through `1017/1016 BC` |
| Historical Temple | `967/966 BC` | supplies the real sanctuary event giving schematic `1016 BC` its meaning |

The controlling correction is:

```text
1446 BC → 721 BC = 725
1446 BC → 586 BC = 860 = 2 × 430.
```

`1876 BC` is the great Genesis / patriarchal Nexus one `430` before the Exodus, not the Exodus itself:

```text
1876 BC → 1446 BC = 430.
```

The repository may describe `1446 BC` cautiously as the primary textually derived Exodus anchor without requiring the stronger claim that modern external history independently proves the date.

## 3. Double- and Triple-`430` Nexus frame

The principal amended frame is:

```text
1876 BC ──430── 1446 BC ──430── 1016 BC ──430── 586 BC.
```

Therefore:

```text
1876 → 1016 = 860 = 2 × 430
1876 →  586 = 1290 = 3 × 430.
```

The first `430` is literal under the active Sojourn state. The following `430` blocks use the declared schematic sanctuary coordinate. Literal and schematic states remain non-collapsed.

The companion Affliction route remains:

```text
1846 → 1446 → 1016 → 586
 400    430    430

1846 − 586 = 1260.
```

## 4. Jacob’s final `70` and localized-LXX `−33`

The standard Jacob rail is:

```text
1929 → 1909 → 1859 BC
  20      50
  total 70.
```

The localized-LXX rail is:

```text
1896 → 1876 → 1826 BC
  20      50
  total 70.
```

The uniform translation is:

```text
1929/1909/1859 − 33
= 1896/1876/1826.
```

The two rails remain distinct from the positive LXX Sojourn division `33 + 397 = 430`.

File_64 locally derives the marriage-field members:

```text
1929 → 1922 → 1909 = 7 + 13
1896 → 1889 → 1876 = 7 + 13.
```

Those `1922/1889 BC` coordinates remain File_64-local and are not inserted into File_04’s Primary anchors or biography tables.

## 5. Jacob–sanctuary–restoration lattice

The full four-level matrix is:

```text
1896 ─20─ 1876 ─50─ 1826
 │430      │430      │430
1466 ─20─ 1446 ─50─ 1396
 │430      │430      │430
1036 ─20─ 1016 ─50─  966
 │430      │430      │430
 606 ─20─  586 ─50─  536.
```

Every horizontal row preserves:

```text
20 + 50 = 70.
```

Every full vertical column preserves:

```text
430 + 430 + 430 = 1290.
```

The generated `1396 BC` coordinate is a matrix member rather than a historical anchor. The `1036/1016/966 BC` row joins Samson’s opening, the schematic sanctuary Nexus, and the historical Temple. The `606/586/536 BC` row joins exile, Jerusalem’s fall, and restoration.

## 6. Jacob–Daniel last-days closure

The amendment places Jacob’s final tribal prophecy beside Daniel’s final revelation while preserving claim-status restraint.

Jacob’s phrase is retained in Hebrew with adjacent translation:

```text
באחרית הימים
“in the latter days.”
```

Daniel’s phrase is retained in Hebrew with adjacent translation:

```text
לקץ הימין
“at the end of the days.”
```

The literary-theological comparison is:

| Jacob | Daniel |
|---|---|
| prophesies over the tribes at the end of his life | receives the final prophecy concerning his people |
| declares what will befall them “in the latter days” | is promised rest and allotted inheritance “at the end of the days” |
| final `70 = 20 + 50` supplies the source rail | exile-restoration `70/69 → 70` supplies the terminal rail |
| deathbed blessing concludes the patriarchal narrative | `1290/1335`, rest, resurrection, and lot conclude the prophetic book |

The relation is literary-theological and providential. It does not prove direct literary dependence.

The file does not claim that Daniel’s lifespan was exactly seventy years. It describes the `606/605 BC → 536 BC` interval as his approximately seventy-year exilic-prophetic career field.

## 7. `606/605 BC → 536 BC = 70/69 → 70`

The two states remain explicit:

```text
606 → 586 → 536 = 20 + 50 = 70

605 → 586 → 536 = 19 + 50 = 69
69 × 70/69 = 70.
```

The clean `20 + 50` row uses `606 BC`, not `605 BC`.

Daniel’s numbers remain non-collapsed:

```text
1290 ≠ 1335
1335 − 1290 = 45.
```

No uncontrolled `45`-year chronology is invented merely to absorb `1335`.

## 8. Cainan–Terah and translated Judges twin-`60` rails

The upper and lower Cainan–Terah rails are:

```text
2151 → 2091 → 2031 BC
  60      60

1936 → 1876 → 1816 BC
  60      60.
```

The two rows differ by `215` at each corresponding position.

The lower rail translates by `860` into the Judges field:

```text
1936 → 1876 → 1816
   −860 each
1076 → 1016 → 956.
```

Therefore:

```text
1936 − 1076 = 860
1876 − 1016 = 860
1816 −  956 = 860.
```

The Judges side is a compositional superposition of the direct and Joshua-extended routes. It does not repeat the Philistine oppression or Samson historically.

## 9. Central palindrome and Exodus-centered radius family

The principal chronological palindrome is:

```text
1936 ─40─ 1896 ─20─ 1876
     ─430─ 1446
     ─430─ 1016 ─20─ 996 ─40─ 956.
```

Its interval sequence is:

```text
40–20–430–430–20–40.
```

The complete same-side BC radius matrix around `1446 BC` is:

| Left coordinate | Radius | Right coordinate | Pair sum |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `1661` | `215` | `1231` | `2892` |
| `1816` | `370` | `1076` | `2892` |
| `1846` | `400` | `1046` | `2892` |
| `1876` | `430` | `1016` | `2892` |
| `1896` | `450` | `996` | `2892` |
| `1936` | `490` | `956` | `2892` |
| `2091` | `645` | `801` | `2892` |

This is same-side symmetry. It is not automatically a formal repository Mirror protocol.

## 10. Joshua-centered triple-`460`

The ladder is:

```text
2396 → 1936 → 1476 → 1016
 460    460    460.
```

Therefore:

```text
2396 − 1016 = 1380 = 3 × 460 = 60 × 23
1380 × 70/69 = 1400
1380 × 25/23 = 1500.
```

The double-`460` portion:

```text
1936 → 1476 → 1016
```

is centered on Joshua’s birth at `1476 BC`, not on the `1446 BC` Exodus.

The `1400` expansion lands at the explicit `996 BC` endpoint:

```text
2396 − 996 = 1400.
```

The `1500` fixed-head endpoint would be `896 BC`; it is not promoted as an active File_64 node.

## 11. Census–Judges palindromes

First-census / explicit-`450` state:

```text
1466 → 1446 → 1406 → 1056 → 1016 → 996
 20      40      350      40      20.
```

Pair sums are `2462`, centered on `1231 BC`.

Second-census / Joshua state:

```text
1426 → 1406 → 1366 → 1016 → 976 → 956
 20      40      350      40     20.
```

Pair sums are `2382`, centered on `1191 BC`.

The two complete coordinate systems translate by exactly `40`.

Numbers counts males twenty years old and upward. `1466/1426 BC` are derived birth-threshold horizons, not “under-twenty” states and not claims that every member of Joshua’s generation died in `1366 BC`.

## 12. Judah `+50` and Israel `+33`

The Judah sanctuary ledger remains principal:

```text
1016 − 966 = 50.
```

The Northern ledger is anchored backward from the historical Fall of Samaria:

```text
963n → 722t = 241.5
963t → 721n = 241.5.
```

Its whole-year comparison is:

```text
963 − 930 = 33.
```

The `+33` is a lesser comparison state and does not replace:

- actual `930 BC`;
- existing `966 → 726 = 240` Samaria route;
- the associated `+36` relation `966 − 930 = 36`;
- Judah’s principal `+50`;
- the historical `967/966 BC` Temple.

The rounded preceding royal comparison is:

```text
1003 → 963 = 40.
```

It overlaps the whole-year coordinate of David’s accession over all Israel after the rounded seven-year Hebron state. Exact `7/7.5` states remain distinct.

## 13. Paired `930` Jacob–construction rails

The standard Jacob rail and Northern construction rail are:

```text
1929 → 1922 → 1909 BC
   7      13

 999 →  992 →  979 BC
   7      13.
```

Coordinate-wise:

```text
1929−999 = 1922−992 = 1909−979 = 930.
```

The localized-LXX Jacob rail and historical construction rail are:

```text
1896 → 1889 → 1876 BC
   7      13

 966 →  959 →  946 BC
   7      13.
```

Coordinate-wise:

```text
1896−966 = 1889−959 = 1876−946 = 930.
```

The Northern `+33` translates the full construction rail:

```text
999−966 = 992−959 = 979−946 = 33.
```

`999/992/979 BC` remain derived comparison coordinates. They do not replace historical `966/959/946 BC`.

## 14. Bethel–Adam typology

Adam lived `930` years and entered a deep sleep before the woman was built from his side.

Jacob slept at Bethel and named the place:

```text
בֵּית אֱלֹהִים
“house of God.”
```

He then entered the wife / household field seven years later.

The `930` arithmetic is fact under the declared rails. The Adam–Jacob relation is typological. It does not prove direct literary dependence and does not generate the dates.

## 15. Crossed `860` routes and double-`400`

The alternative single-`33` routes are:

```text
1859 − 999 = 860
1826 − 966 = 860.
```

The ordinary unadjusted relation is:

```text
1859 − 966 = 893 = 860 + 33.
```

Applying both adjustments simultaneously is not active:

```text
1826 − 999 = 827.
```

The land / Temple companions are:

```text
1399 − 999 = 400
1366 − 966 = 400.
```

`1399 BC` is the land-rest state. `1366 BC` is Joshua’s death state; it is not asserted as the exact death date of every member of his generation.

## 16. Subordinate Flood / Noah / Shem corroboration

The Flood material is explicitly secondary:

```text
2456 → 1016 = 1440 = 4 × 360
2516 → 1016 = 1500.
```

Partitions:

```text
2516 → 2166 → 1016
      350  + 1150
      = 1500

2516 → 2016 → 1016
      500  + 1000
      = 1500.
```

`2456 BC` is the Gear-1 Flood / Arphaxad comparison state. `2516 BC` is its propagated `+60` comparison. `2166 BC` is Abraham’s birth in the primary File_64 argument and a Noah-death shared label only in the named comparison. `2016 BC` is a Shem-death comparison label.

This block demonstrates the wider importance of `1016 BC`; it does not carry the Judges proof or become a second principal Nexus route.

## 1. Research problem and file disposition

File_06 already preserved a compact Jephthah bridge:

```text
1406 BC → 1106 BC = 300
12600 + 300 = 12900
4116 BC → 1106 BC = 3010 = 7 × 430.
```

The new discussion established that Judges 11:26 concerns the east-Jordan territorial field involving Heshbon, Aroer, the Arnon, Sihon, Moab, and Balak. File_64 therefore distinguishes:

```text
1407 BC → 1107 BC = 300
1406 BC → 1107 BC = 299
1406 BC → 1106 BC = 300.
```

File_06 remains the compact bridge and index file. File_64 becomes the full applied list–chronology and sanctuary-reconciliation proof.

## 2. Historical-theological and compositional-theological chronology

File_64 preserves two simultaneous theological registers.

| Register | Function |
|---|---|
| Historical-theological chronology | Events, oppressions, regional judgeships, rests, and deliverance activity as they may overlap in actual history. |
| Compositional-theological duration ledger | Expressly stated year values counted under a declared operator because the attached numerals form a literary-theological structure. |

The second register is not treated as a failed literal chronology. The historical and schematic uses are complementary but non-collapsed.

## 3. Jephthah’s east- and west-Jordan key

The primary rails are:

```text
1407 BC → 1107 BC = 300

1406 BC → 1107 BC
= 299
= 13 × 23

299 × 300/299 = 300.
```

The adjacent completed-boundary companion remains:

```text
1406 BC → 1106 BC = 300.
```

Node-class control:

| Coordinate | Function |
|---:|---|
| `1107 BC` | Jephthah speech / accession state. |
| `1106 BC` | Completed-boundary / cumulative harmonic companion. |

The one-year transfer preserves the outer west-Jordan span:

```text
299 + 91 = 300 + 90 = 390.
```

## 4. Post-Jephthah duration tail

The source-order tail is:

```text
Jephthah              6
Ibzan                  7
Elon                  10
Abdon                  8
Philistine oppression 40
Samson                 20
                     ───
                      91
```

Therefore:

```text
6 + 7 + 10 + 8 = 31
40 + 20 = 60
31 + 60 = 91
91 = 7 × 13.
```

The direct landing is:

```text
1107 − 91 = 1016.
```

The west- and east-Jordan totals are:

```text
299 + 91 = 390 = 13 × 30
300 + 91 = 391 = 17 × 23.
```

## 5. Complete Judges duration ledger

The bounded Judges 3:8–16:31 corpus contains eighteen expressly stated year-periods.

Subjugation / oppression:

```text
8 + 18 + 20 + 7 + 18 + 40 = 111.
```

Rest / rule / judgeship:

```text
40 + 80 + 40 + 40 + 3 + 23 + 22 + 6 + 7 + 10 + 8 + 20
= 299
= 13 × 23.
```

Complete printed-duration ledger:

```text
111 + 299 = 410.
```

Jephthah’s retrospective `300` is the interpretive key and is not counted as a nineteenth serial duration. Samson’s repeated `20` is counted once. The text’s statement that Samson judged in the days of the Philistines preserves the historical overlap while the compositional ledger retains both printed values.

## 6. The three-position `1016 BC` convergence

### 6.1 Direct Jephthah route

```text
1446 → 1406 = 40
1406 → 1107 = 299
1107 → 1016 = 91

40 + 299 + 91 = 430.
```

Here `1016 BC` is the closure of Samson’s `20`.

### 6.2 Explicit `450` ledger

```text
40 wilderness + 410 Judges = 450
1446 − 450 = 996.
```

Since:

```text
450 − 20 = 430,
```

`1016 BC` is the joint between the Philistine `40` and Samson’s `20`.

### 6.3 Joshua-extended `490` ledger

```text
40 wilderness
+ 40 Joshua post-Conquest overlay
+ 410 Judges
= 490.
```

Since:

```text
490 − 60 = 430,
```

`1016 BC` is the opening of the Philistine `40`.

### 6.4 Signature table

| Route | Complete schematic tally | Complete endpoint | Position of `1016 BC` |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Joshua-extended | `490` | `956 BC` | Opening of Philistine `40` |
| Explicit printed-duration ledger | `450` | `996 BC` | Joint between Philistine `40` and Samson `20` |
| Jephthah direct | `430` | `1016 BC` | Closure of Samson `20` |

The three routes are complementary theological ledgers, not rival literal histories.

## 7. First Samuel, monarchy, and construction recapitulation

Judges closes with:

```text
Philistine subjugation 40
Samson                  20
                       ──
                        60.
```

First Samuel numerically begins with the same form:

```text
Eli                     40
Kiriath-jearim marker   20
                       ──
                        60.
```

The Kiriath-jearim marker follows the ark’s return from Philistine territory. It is not a twenty-year wait before the return.

The two `60` modules compare with the schematic royal register:

```text
(40 + 20) + (40 + 20)
= 120
= 40 + 40 + 40
```

for Saul, David, and Solomon.

The Temple and royal-house construction envelope is:

```text
7 + 13 = 20.
```

The `450` Judges route independently reproduces the schematic Temple–palace pair:

```text
1016 → 996 = 20,
```

beside the historical-rounded pair:

```text
966 → 946 = 20.
```

## 8. Judges 17–21 as the negative image of Jerusalem

The unnumbered epilogue attaches compositionally to the terminal schematic coordinate but is not dated historically to `1016 BC`.

Its negative sanctuary field includes:

- Micah’s private house of gods;
- the hired Bethlehem Levite;
- the Danite seizure and rival sanctuary;
- the second Bethlehem–Levite narrative;
- Jebus / Jerusalem rejected as alien;
- Gibeah selected and exposed as covenantally corrupt;
- the house of God, ark, Phinehas, Bethel, and Shiloh;
- the repeated absence-of-a-king formula.

The later canonical resolution is:

| Judges disorder | Davidic–Solomonic resolution |
|---|---|
| Bethlehem Levite serving counterfeit worship | David comes from Bethlehem |
| Jebus rejected | David captures Jebus |
| No king | Davidic kingship |
| Rival shrines and fragmented worship | Jerusalem central sanctuary |
| House of gods | House of the LORD |
| Tribal civil war | United monarchy |

The literary theology interprets the already established arithmetic coordinate; it does not generate the arithmetic retroactively.

## 9. Great and lesser Nexuses

The principal frame is:

```text
1876 BC ──430── 1446 BC ──430── 1016 BC.
```

Node classes:

| Coordinate | Function |
|---:|---|
| `1876 BC` | Great Genesis / Pentateuchal convergence Nexus. |
| `1446 BC` | Exodus fulcrum and midpoint. |
| `1016 BC` | Lesser schematic Judges–Kings–Jerusalem sanctuary Nexus. |

Therefore:

```text
1876 − 1016
= 860
= 430 + 430.
```

The Triple-`430` extension is:

```text
1876 → 1446 → 1016 → 586
 430    430    430

1876 − 586 = 1290.
```

The companion Affliction route is:

```text
1846 → 1446 → 1016 → 586
 400    430    430

1846 − 586 = 1260.
```

Thus:

```text
1260 + 30 = 1290
12600 + 300 = 12900.
```

## 10. Historical Temple branch

The schematic and historical routes remain distinct:

```text
1876 → 1016 = 860 = 430 + 430
1876 →  966 = 910 = 430 + 480

910 − 860 = 50.
```

The historical Temple foundation remains `967/966 BC`. The schematic `1016 BC` Nexus mathematically reconciles the Judges and Kings ledgers, while the actual Jerusalem Temple supplies the event through which the schematic coordinate receives its sanctuary meaning.

The ordered construction pairs remain:

```text
schematic:          1016/996 BC
historical-rounded:  966/946 BC.
```

## 11. Joshua, David, and the outer envelope

Joshua’s birth supplies:

```text
1476 − 1016
= 460
= 20 × 23.
```

The same double-`430` corridor is repartitioned as:

```text
1876 → 1476 → 1016
 400      460

400 + 460 = 860 = 430 + 430.
```

The Joshua–David giant-conflict comparison remains typological: Joshua attacks the Anakim while Gath remains; David defeats Goliath of Gath and the Davidic warriors later defeat the remaining giant descendants.

The civil outer envelope is:

```text
2166 BC ──720── 1446 BC ──430── 1016 BC ──430── 586 BC ──720── AD 135.
```

Therefore:

```text
2166 → 1016 = 1150
1016 BC → AD 135 = 1150
1150 + 1150 = 2300
720 + 430 + 430 + 720 = 2300.
```

The AD 135 arm remains providential synchronization, not proof of conscious biblical projection.

## 12. Appendix-only `111/299` category projections

The appendix preserves the following parallel phase-aware projections:

```text
1407t/1406n − 111
= 1296t/1295n

1407t/1406n − 299
= 1108t/1107n

1407t/1406n − 300
= 1107t/1106n.
```

The cumulative comparisons are:

```text
14036 → 1296 = 12740 = 35 × 364
14006 → 1406 = 12600 = 35 × 360

12740 − 12600
= 140
= 35 × 4.
```

The Danielic cumulative rails include:

```text
14008 → 1108
= 14007 → 1107
= 14006 → 1106
= 12900.
```

The category-major sequential corollary is retained as appendix-only:

```text
111 + 300 = 411
1407 − 411 = 996

1296 − 280 = 1016
1016 − 20 = 996.
```

The argument is plausible scribal design but non-load-bearing because it depends on category reordering, Enochian completion, and separation of Samson’s final `20`.

## 1. File disposition and dependency direction

This translation notation is not a new Gear, clutch, Mirror protocol, or repository-wide operator.

## 2. Scale-neutral carrier theorem

File_63 begins from the ordered whole-year / quarter-phase pair:

```text
40 / 40.25.
```

The principal multiplier ladder is:

| Multiplier | Whole-year body | Quarter-phase body | Difference |
|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| `3` | `120` | `120.75` | `0.75` |
| `10` | `400` | `402.5` | `2.5` |
| `12` | `480` | `483` | `3` |
| `30` | `1200` | `1207.5` | `7.5` |
| `300` | `12000` | `12075` | `75` |

The three carrier classes remain distinct:

```text
480=12×40
483=12×40.25=3×161=69×7
490=483×70/69.
```

The phase expansion is exact:

```text
483−480=3=12×0.25.
```

The scale relation is orientation-neutral as arithmetic but chronology-sensitive in application. Multiplication or division preserves the ratio; it does not identify historical dates, node-classes, traditions, or operators.

## 3. Twelve male units, Levi’s thirteenth, and the Conquest hinge

The whole-year `480` carrier is read simultaneously as:

```text
12×40 years
12×(7+33) male-completion units.
```

This is a ledger of completed units. It does not date Jacob’s sons forty years apart.

The proposed Levi rail is:

```text
1919n→1439n=480=12×40
1439n→1399n=40
1919n→1399n=520=13×40.
```

The enclosing field is:

```text
1926→1366=560=14×40
560=7+[13×40]+33.
```

The Conquest supplies the inverse hinge:

```text
1439→1406→1399=33+7
1406→1399→1366=7+33.
```

Thus the shared Conquest/rest interval closes one Levitical forty and opens Joshua’s final forty without moving the `1406 BC`, `1399 BC`, or `1366 BC` source states.

## 4. Jethro, Exodus, Conquest, and Joshua

The same-side field is:

```text
1486→1446→1406=40+40.
```

`1486 BC` is the Midian-priestly formation threshold: Moses enters the household of Jethro, the non-Levitical priest of Midian. Jethro and Melchizedek may be compared as non-Levitical priestly witnesses; the file does not claim identical office, genealogy, or textual order.

The two forties may be displayed as parallel male forms:

```text
(7+33)+(7+33)=80,
```

or as an Exodus-centered inversion:

```text
33+7 | 7+33=80=66+14.
```

Joshua’s regular death at `1366 BC` and cumulative endpoint at `1296 BC` remain distinct node-classes separated by `70` years.

## 5. Abraham–Temple decuple birth law

The principal same-side macro body is:

```text
2166→2026→1366→1296→966 BC
 140   660   70   330.
```

Therefore:

```text
140+660+70+330=1200
(140, 660, 70, 330)=10×(14, 66, 7, 33)
800+400=10×(80+40).
```

Isaac’s marriage at age `40` supplies the internal knot:

```text
100+40=140=2×70.
```

The established Abraham–Exodus–Temple body is refactored without alteration:

```text
720+480=800+400=1200.
```

The five-component Mosaic comparison is:

```text
0.75+14+66+7+33=120.75.
```

At tenfold scale:

```text
140+660+70+330+7.5=1207.5.
```

The gestational member moves from the head of the Mosaic body to the tail of the Abraham–Temple body. The Mirror later reverses the order again; the same-side proof does not depend upon that AD completion.

## 6. Temple formation and the `161` quotient

Phase-expanding thirty Mosaic units gives:

```text
30×40=1200
30×40.25=1207.5
1207.5−1200=7.5.
```

The exact Temple construction interval supplies the same `7.5`-year difference. Under the Prophetic `360`-day register:

```text
7.5×360=2700=10×270.
```

The gestational comparison is typological / calendrical. First Kings does not state `2700` literal construction days.

The invariant quotient is:

```text
120.75/0.75
=1207.5/7.5
=12075/75
=161.
```

Since `161=7×23`, the scale ladder is joined to the File_60/File_62 elemental field without making every refactorization an independent proof.

## 7. Precise and Rounded United-Monarchy correction

The author clarified that the literal / precise phase rails are:

```text
Nisan:  1050n→1010n→970n→930n BC
Tishri: 1050t→1010t→970t→930t BC.
```

Every same-phase reign is forty years:

```text
1050−1010=40
1010−970=40
970−930=40.
```

David’s exact cross-phase reign remains:

```text
1010n→970t=40.5=7.5+33.
```

David’s rounded whole-year display remains:

```text
7+33=40.
```

The distinct Rounded Scaffold rail is:

```text
1051n→1011n→971n→931n BC.
```

The former `1011→971 BC` field is therefore Rounded / mod-5, not the literal / precise United-Monarchy rail.

The Rounded Aaron/Moses locks are:

```text
1531n−971n=560=14×40
1526n−966n=560=14×40.
```

The shared `966n BC` label retains two distinct functions:

```text
1446n−966n=480
```

as the cardinal historical Exodus-to-Temple landing, and:

```text
1526n−966n=560
```

as a Rounded Moses-to-Temple comparison. Shared year-label does not create state identity.

On the precise Tishri display:

```text

```

```text

970t→930t=40.
```

Therefore:

```text
920×25/23=1000.
```


This is the final measured pre-kingship phase, not David’s entire flight from Saul.

The precise half-year joint is:

```text
970n→970t=0.5 year.
```

Its interpretation as an exact co-rule-to-sole-rule transition remains proposed because the source does not state a six-month duration.

## 1. Research problem and file disposition

Final File_61 established the full fourteen-year cumulative clutch but intentionally left several consequences outside its first proof spine to avoid excessive complexity. The subsequent discussion reopened those consequences under a controlled companion-file architecture.

File_62 does not excuse or replace the correction of File_61. The bounded File_61 amendment was completed after File_62 Finalization.

## 2. The half-clutch theorem

The full File_61 clutch is:

```text
T14(C) = C − 14
```

File_62 proves its exact midpoint:

```text
H7(C) = C − 7
H7(C) = [C + T14(C)] ÷ 2
```

Therefore:

```text
C → H7(C) → T14(C)
       7          7
```

The Moses/Nisan rails are:

| Boundary | Original cumulative | Half clutch | Full clutch |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Abraham | `2435` | `2428` | `2421` |
| Isaac | `2260` | `2253` | `2246` |
| Jacob | `2080` | `2073` | `2066` |
| Levi | `1933` | `1926` | `1919` |
| Kohath | `1796` | `1789` | `1782` |
| Amram | `1663` | `1656` | `1649` |
| Moses-side interface | `1526` | `1519` | `1512` |

The same uniform translation preserves the source cumulative lifespans. The `H7` rail is not an independent manuscript chronology and does not move Moses, Exodus, Conquest, Aaron’s terminal state, or Joshua’s `1296 BC` cumulative endpoint.

## 3. Male and female birth-law interface

The half-clutch yields the male form:

```text
         7       33
         total 40
```

The full clutch yields the female form:

```text

         total 80
```

The regrouping identity is exact:

```text
2 × (7 + 33)
= (7 + 7) + (33 + 33)
= 14 + 66
= 80
```

The File_61/File_62 chronology supplies the year-scale application. File_14 remains controlling for Leviticus 12’s birth-law vocabulary. Jubilees 3 supplies explicit ancient reception-history precedent for applying those periods to Adam–Eve chronology.

## 4. Joseph’s inherited cumulative portions

File_61’s original Joseph branches are fixed-offset constructions:

```text
forward from Levi:   L − 110
backward from Kohath: K + 110
```

Uniform translation therefore commutes with the `110`-year offset:

```text
T14(C ± 110) = T14(C) ± 110
H7(C ± 110)  = H7(C) ± 110
```

The principal Moses/Nisan rails are:

| Joseph portion | Original | Half clutch | Full clutch |

| Backward from Kohath | `1906 → 1796` | `1899 → 1789` | `1892 → 1782` |

Every portion remains exactly `110` years. Joseph remains a collateral double portion and is not inserted into the genealogical Levi–Kohath trunk.

The full-clutch forward comparison with Joseph’s actual regular `1915–1805 BC` biography yields:

```text
1922 → 1919 → 1918 → 1915
        3       1       3
```

and the corresponding terminal week:

```text
        3       3       1
```

with:

The detailed `27/83/110`, `20/23`, `130/133`, and cyclic triple-`110` proof remains File_62-controlled.

## 5. Why the fourteen-year clutch lands efficiently

The discussion clarified that `14` is already embedded in both regular and cumulative architecture:

```text
Jacob 147 − Kohath 133 = 14
Levi / Amram 137 − Aaron 123 = 14
Abraham 175 − Jacob 147 = 28 = 14 + 14
```

The heptadic lifespan ladder is:

```text
133 → 147 → 161 → 175
      +14    +14    +14
```

or:

```text
19×7 → 21×7 → 23×7 → 25×7
```

The regular narrative supplies additional double-heptads through Ishmael–Isaac, Jacob’s two wife-service periods, and the Plenty/Famine field. The cumulative chronology stacks the same textual lifespans end-to-end. The clutch is therefore best described as a local conversion between stacked lifespan volume and overlapping historical placement.

The identity:

```text
133 + 137 = 270
147 + 123 = 270
```

shows the transfer of `14` without changing the total. The Kohath–Amram pair preserves the gestational `270 = 9 × 30` on original, half-clutched, and fully clutched rails.

## 7. Moses’ phase pair and the dual-`490` carrier

File_62 proposes the phase-paired states:

```text
Jacob at Bethel / Haran: 1930t/1929n BC
Aaron birth:             1530t/1529n BC
Moses birth:             1527t/1526n BC
plague opening / Exodus: 1447t/1446n BC
```

The same-phase ages preserve Moses `80` and Aaron `83`, while the cross-phase field permits `80.5` and the ordinary `3` / phase-resolved `3.5` distinction.

The Jacob-to-Exodus carrier is:

```text
483 = 402.5 + 40.25 + 40.25
    = 12 × 40.25
    = 3 × 161
    = 69 × 7
```

Two distinct operations reach `490`:

```text
local Priestly route:
402.5 + (80.5 × 25/23) = 490
```

```text
whole Prophetic route:
483 × 70/69 = 490
```

The full Priestly expansion is:

```text
483 × 25/23 = 525
```

and the day-volume closure is exact:

```text
525 × 336 = 490 × 360 = 176400
```

The equality is a total-day equivalence. It does not identify the Priestly `336`-day year with the Prophetic `360`-day year or make a physical solar-calendar identity exact.

The first `70` ends at Jacob’s death, immediately after his prophetic blessings over the twelve sons, and is followed by the textual `70` days of mourning. Jacob’s age `77` at flight from his brother supplies a bounded reception-history comparison with Matthew 18’s `77 / 70×7` saying concerning forgiveness of a brother.

These comparisons are canonical / typological corroboration, not proof that Daniel 9 directly refers to Jacob.

with:

## 10. Book of Jubilees hermeneutical warrant and reconstruction method

The research established two distinct uses of Jubilees.

### 10.1 Explicit ancient numerical hermeneutic

Jubilees 3 explicitly derives the male and female purification periods from Adam’s and Eve’s timed entrance into Eden:

```text
male:   7 + 33 = 40
female: 14 + 66 = 80
```

The text then moves into seven Edenic years and a precisely dated Fall. Jubilees’ Flood occurs on the same month/day anniversary `1300` years later:

```text
Creation → Fall = 7
Fall → Flood    = 1300
Creation → Flood = 1307 = 7 + 1300
```

This does not prove direct scribal construction of every MT alignment. It demonstrates that day/year and legal/chronological number transfer belongs to an attested ancient Jewish hermeneutic.

### 10.2 Spine-first / divergence-second reconstruction

For BJ’s internally conflicting Isaac–Jacob sector, the agreed method is:

1. lock BJ’s stated Abraham anchor and explicit biographical totals;
2. reconstruct the inherited spine;
3. preserve contradictory surface jubilee dates separately;
4. analyze the numerical purpose of the divergence.

The reconstructed spine is:

```text
Abraham 1981
Isaac   1881   (+100)
Jacob   1821   (+60)
Entry   1691   (+130)
```

This makes Jacob `79` at BJ’s retained `1742 BC` Bethel coordinate. The displacement from the MT’s `77` is informative because BJ’s Bethel date produces the overt `336` to Conquest and `343 = 7 × 49` to land-rest structures.

The archive records the methodological contrast:

> BJ exposes the algorithm; the MT integrates it.

BJ’s inconsistencies are therefore useful evidence of its overt heptadic priorities, provided the inherited spine is locked before the divergences are interpreted.

## 1. File_61 purpose and relation to File_60

File_60 established the Levitical Covenant Spine through the regular, localized `−33`, Mirror, and cumulative witnesses to the `147/161/175`, `299/161/460`, and `579600` structures.

File_61 asks a different question:

> How do regular chronology, its principal variant states, and cumulative lifespan chronology weave together as one chronological system from Jacob through Moses, with Joseph’s collateral branch closing through Joshua?

The file therefore functions as the repository’s direct cumulative–regular interface file. File_60 remains the immediate parent and the controller of the Levitical Covenant Spine synthesis. File_61 does not rederive the File_59 census totals or replace File_22’s cumulative arithmetic, File_03 / File_04 regular biographies, or File_18’s chronology tables.

The governing inverse-tree metaphor is:

```text
Jacob through Moses / Joshua = dense root zone
Isaac                         = root-to-trunk graft
Isaac through Adam            = macro-harmonic cumulative trunk
```

The metaphor is explanatory only and is not an arithmetic operator.

## 3. Two operating zones of the cumulative chronology

File_61 distinguishes two coordinated cumulative regimes.

### 3.1 Direct-overlay root corridor

From cumulative Jacob through Moses, cumulative coordinates enter the same historical centuries as regular patriarchal and Egyptian chronology. Under declared state control, the `+215`, `−215`, localized `−33`, ordinary `3`, phase-resolved `3.5`, and hidden Kohath–Amram begetting-age field permit direct cumulative–regular comparison.

The principal root atlas is:

| Figure / boundary | Moses/Nisan cumulative | Aaron/Tishri cumulative | Principal regular comparison |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| Jacob | `2080 BC` | `2083.5 BC` | Jacob `2006 BC`; Ishmael shared label at `2080 BC` |
| Levi | `1933 BC` | `1936.5 BC` | Jacob age-`70/77` field; Levi regular biography |
| Joseph backward portion | `1906 BC` | `1909.5 BC` | Benjamin / return-to-Canaan field |
| Joseph forward portion | `1823 BC` | `1826.5 BC` | second Joseph `110` portion |
| Kohath | `1796 BC` | `1799.5 BC` | Judah / Levi death field |
| Amram | `1663 BC` | `1666.5 BC` | `−215` Plenty/Famine field |
| Aaron / Moses | `1526 BC` | `1529.5 BC` | regular birth field |
| terminal | `1406n BC` | `1407t BC` | deaths / Conquest |
| Joshua cumulative extension | `1296 BC` | dependency-controlled comparison | Joshua regular `1476–1366 BC` |

Shared labels never erase node-class distinctions.

### 3.2 Adam–Isaac macro-harmonic trunk

By cumulative Isaac, dense event-level overlay with regular chronology becomes less stable. The cumulative system remains highly ordered, but its principal expression becomes long-span harmonic projection:

```text
4836–4829 BC → 1406–1399 BC = 3430 = 7 × 7 × 70
4836 BC → 536 BC            = 4300 = 10 × 430
5436 BC → 536 BC            = 4900 = 70 × 70
14006 BC → 1406 BC          = 12600
```

The direct roots converge toward Moses; the macro trunk extends backward toward Adam.

## 4. Jacob–Levi `147` and the `70/77` lattice

The cumulative Jacob–Levi blocks are:

```text
2083.5 − 1936.5 = 147
2080   − 1933   = 147
```

Regular Jacob’s lifespan is divided in both orders:

```text
2006 → 1936 → 1859 = 70 + 77 = 147
2006 → 1929 → 1859 = 77 + 70 = 147
```

Its exact midpoint is:

```text
147 ÷ 2 = 73.5
```

The age-`70` and age-`77` nodes stand `3.5` years on either side of the midpoint. After Jacob’s death, the traditional Judah and regular Levi death coordinates repeat the two measures:

```text
1859 − 1789 = 70
1859 − 1782 = 77
```

Judah’s `129`-year lifespan is a traditional comparison witness and is not treated as explicit canonical lifespan data.

## 5. Cumulative Levi and the divided Danielic week

The whole-year cumulative-Levi-to-Exodus anatomy is:

```text
1936 → 1929 =   7
1929 → 1782 = 147
1782 → 1446 = 336
──────────────────
1936 → 1446 = 490
```

At the coefficient level:

```text
1 × 7 + 21 × 7 + 48 × 7 = 70 × 7
```

The phase field gives:

```text
483   = 69 × 7
486.5 = 69.5 × 7
490   = 70 × 7
```

The Aaron/Moses `3/3.5` branch divides the seventieth week without treating whole-year and exact-phase displays as corrections of one another.

The recognized calendar equivalence remains:

```text
7 × 360 = 7.5 × 336 = 2520
```

No new rule was created by using coordinated `7/7.5` and `3/3.5` states.

## 6. Hidden Kohath–Amram ages and the maximum envelopes

Genesis gives begetting ages throughout the Adam-to-patriarch sequence, whereas Exodus 6 supplies only the complete lifespans of Levi, Kohath, and Amram. File_61 argues that this textual silence preserves the elasticity needed for the `215` and `430` Egypt registers to remain complementary.

The maximum non-overlap envelopes are:

```text
Levi birth-to-Exodus maximum volume:
137 + 133 + 137 + 80 = 487
actual elapsed span       = 473
surplus                   = 14
```

```text
Entry-side maximum:
94 + 133 + 137 + 80 = 444
actual Egypt span         = 430
surplus                   = 14
```

```text
Levi-death-side maximum:
```

The values `487`, `444`, and `350` are extremal lifespan envelopes, not literal father-at-death histories.

## 7. The fourteen-year clutch

The repeated surplus defines the File_61-local clutch:

```text
T14(C) = C − 14
```

Because BC labels decrease forward in time, subtracting `14` advances a coordinate by fourteen years.

The formal stages are:

```text
original block:
P − S = L
```

```text
one-boundary engagement:
(P − 14) − S = L − 14
```

```text
two-boundary propagation:
(P − 14) − (S − 14) = L
```

The complete one-boundary output is disclosed:

```text
161 | 166 | 133 | 123 | 119 | 123 | 66
```

The mechanically unremarkable `166` and `119` remain visible and are not assigned forced interpretations.

The translated Moses/Nisan rail is:

| Original cumulative | Translated coordinate | Principal landing |
|---:|---:|---|
| Abraham `2435` | `2421` | clutch head for `175 → 161 → 175` |
| Isaac `2260` | `2246` | Priestly–Prophetic register exchange |
| Jacob `2080` | `2066` | regular Isaac birth |
| Levi `1933` | `1919` | regular Levi birth |
| Kohath `1796` | `1782` | regular Levi death |
| Amram `1663` | `1649` | extremal Kohath-death / Amram-beginning boundary |
| Moses `1526` | `1512` | extremal Amram-death / purification pivot |

Shifting both boundaries preserves the original cumulative intervals:

```text
175 | 180 | 147 | 137 | 133 | 137
```

The clutch is local to the declared Abraham–Moses cumulative trunk and is not promoted to a global manuscript or repository-wide chronology variant.

The two-generation cumulative measures are:

These are arithmetic facts. Reading Egypt as Israel’s gestational chamber and the Exodus as national birth is typological / theological interpretation.

This is compared with the female-child purification form in Leviticus 12, Moses’ conception / birth / nursing rhetoric in Numbers 11:12, and Israel / Jerusalem as woman and bride in Ezekiel 16.

The male `7 + 33 = 40` remains a secondary comparison because its intermediate point lacks the independent extremal derivation possessed by `1512 BC`.

## 2. File_60 authoring and Finalization

### 2.1 File identity and purpose

### 2.2 Human-facing entry and plausible scribal layer

The file begins with the simplest proposed scribal relation:

```text
579600 = 1260 × 460
       = 161 × 3600
```

The factorization is exposed through values already native to the census and chronology:

```text
1260 = 7 × 180
460  = 20 × 23

7 × 23   = 161
180 × 20 = 3600
```

The approved symbolic restatement is:

```text
579600 = 100 × (6 × 161 × 6)
6 × 161 × 6 = 5796
```

The `6–161–6` form is registered as an author-defined palindromic enclosure at `100×` scale. It is not a new operator and not independent proof.

The direct `579600 = 161 × 3600` integration is classified as plausible simple scribal design. The larger Mirror and cumulative network is classified more cautiously as structural inference, typological reading, or providential over-intention.

### 2.3 Regular birth and death spines

```text
expanded route  = 175

   +14   +14
```

The principal death spine is:

```text

161 + 161 = 322
```

This is the immediate chronological warrant for the `161` factor in `579600 = 161 × 3600`.

## Original two-census architecture

The military-census totals remain:

```markdown
Numbers 1 first census       603550
Numbers 26 second census     601730
Mean                         602640
Full difference                1820 = 5 × 364
Half-difference                  910 = 2.5 × 364 = 13 × 70
```

The primary anchor application is:

```markdown
1446 + 602640 = 604086 BC
1466 + 602640 = 604106 BC
1476 + 602640 = 604116 BC
```

The rounded contraction is:

```markdown
604086 − 600000 = 4086 BC
604106 − 600000 = 4106 BC
604116 − 600000 = 4116 BC
```

The principal decomposition is:

```markdown
602640 = 144000 + 458640
458640 = 1274 × 360 = 1260 × 364
```

The two placements of `144000` form the commutative census square:

```markdown
1446 + 144000 = 145446 BC
604086 − 144000 = 460086 BC
604086 − 145446 = 460086 − 1446 = 458640
```

## Babel–Sinai–Zion and the `±910` rail

The two-census half-difference generates:

```markdown
2356 BC → 1446 BC → 536 BC
       910       910
```

The full width remains:

```markdown
2356 − 536 = 1820 = 5 × 364
```

The parallel `+30` rail is:

```markdown
2386 BC → 1476 BC → 566 BC
```

The file preserves `2356 BC` as the rounded Peleg / Babel / Table-of-Nations state, `1446 BC` as the Exodus / first-census anchor horizon, and `537/536 BC` as the returnee-census / Second-Temple-foundation field. `Zion` is a theological sanctuary designation rather than a claim that Ezra 3 uses that word in the relevant verse.

## Genesis 46 and flock-reception controls

The maternal household totals remain:

```markdown
Leah 33
Zilpah 16
Rachel 14
Bilhah 7
Total 70
```

Controlled internal arrangements include:

```markdown
33 + 16 = 49
14 + 7 = 21
49 + 21 = 70

33 + 7 = 40
16 + 14 = 30
40 + 30 = 70

33 → 49 → 63 → 70
```

Genesis 33 supplies the textually grounded three-group order:

```markdown
handmaids 23 → Leah 33 → Rachel 14
23 + 33 + 14 = 70
```

All attempts to convert the household `70` or its internal arrangements into dates are appendix-only speculative future-study material. The arithmetic is preserved; authorial intention is not asserted.

Reception-history control:

- 1 Enoch 89 represents Jacob, Joseph, the eleven brothers, and their descendants as sheep / flocks within a sustained animal apocalypse.
- This establishes symbolic flock encoding of Jacob's household in a Second Temple Jewish framework.
- It does not state that the flock number equals years.
- Genesis 41 remains the explicit biblical animal-year precedent through the good and bad cows.

Lexical handling:

- Rachel's name meaning `ewe` is retained as a lexical datum.
- Leah's proposed `cow / wild cow` etymology remains disputed and non-load-bearing.
- Rachel's household `14 = 7 + 7` contains Joseph, interpreter of the `7 + 7` cow-years; Bilhah's household `7` is the single-heptad companion. This remains structural inference, not proof.

## Chronology and phase decisions

The exact cumulative Jacob equation is canonical:

```markdown
2083.5 BC → 1446.5 BC = 637 = 13 × 49
```

The unsuffixed `2083 BC → 1446 BC` form is retained only as source-history shorthand.

The phase-resolved Nisan spine is:

## File_02 — §0.7–§0.12 pass, finalization, pressure-test, and refresh records

```markdown
### 0.7 Arithmetic Pass scope and result

Arithmetic Pass scope:
This pass checks same-side BC spans by subtraction, same-side range spans by subtraction, civil cross-axis spans by `BC + AD − 1`, exact factorization claims, Key-of-23 whole-year conversions, SKL beginning-and-anchor checks, Flood Week boundary checks, and MT four-position matrix shifts.

The Arithmetic Pass did not pressure test the file, recompute `File_34` SKL/Berossus derivations, or resolve dependency-controlled month-state readings.

Arithmetic Pass result:
All locally executable arithmetic statements in the main body check under their stated operators. Items that remain open are dependency-control issues, not corrected arithmetic errors. The retained audit items are listed in §8.6.

### 0.8 Argument Pass scope and result

Argument Pass scope:
This pass clarifies the evidential role of each claim without changing arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, operators, sign conventions, ranges, slash-pairs, theological claims, or cross-file dependencies.

Argument-control rule:
The file proceeds in this evidential order:

| Rank | Evidence type | Function in `File_02` |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Textual datum and arithmetic fact | Establish the dates, spans, ratios, and state boundaries |
| 2 | Structural inference | Relate repeated Flood, Key-of-23, cumulative, BJ, and SKL alignments |
| 3 | Shared-tradition evidence | Support comparison between SP, BJ/Jubilees, and wider Second Temple chronological grammar |
| 4 | Typological note | Explain theological fit, especially Shem, Japheth, Flood judgment, and AD 70 |
| 5 | Providential synchronization | Classify coherence that exceeds demonstrable authorial intent |
| 6 | Dependency note | Preserve material controlled by `File_18`, `File_22`, `File_34`, `File_51a`, `File_54`, or Restart Capsule v11.12 |

Argument Pass result:
The main argument is now arranged as follows. The Chronological Matrix establishes the controlled Flood states. The Logic Engine states the regular inter-tradition harmonics. The 5290 axis and Sumerian-Christological bridge state same-side BC spans. The Judgment Harmonic states the cross-axis envelope. The Shem ±2 section explains localized versus propagated anomaly states. The BJ/Jubilees section supplies shared-tradition comparison without claiming direct dependence.

Placement decision:
The SKL Short / Long register remains in the main body because `File_02` is a Sumerian-integration file and because the local SKL state labels are needed to prevent later confusion between Sumerian A, Sumerian B, `+30`, `+2`, and `+720`. Full SKL/Berossus proof remains dependency-controlled by `File_34`.

Placement decision:
The SKL +30 / `36 BC` rail remains in the main body as a secondary rail clarification. It does not carry the primary Solar / Enochian split, but it prevents the +30 Apparent Mode from being misread as a failed 6 BC calculation.

### 0.9 Author-Decision Pass scope and result

Author-Decision Pass scope:
This pass resolves audit notes that can now be settled from controlling exemplars, prior author approvals, and explicit dependency boundaries. It does not perform Finalization, pressure testing, new arithmetic discovery, or a full dependency-file rewrite.

Author-Decision Pass result:
The Noah row in the localized MT Minimum-Regular Noah/Shem pivot table is corrected to the `File_00` controlling baseline: Noah `2843/2841 BC`, death `1893/1891 BC`. This correction is not silent; the source-preservation note is retained in §6.2. The correction aligns the Noah pair with the local 600/598 Flood-age state and with the 598 ↔ 600 gear-shift section.

The LXX Long Count `3242 BC` row remains dependency-controlled as a preserved display state. It is not used in the local `780 = 6 × 130` calculation, which remains governed by LXX Regular-Minimum `3023 BC`.

The SKL Short / Long appended `3 months and 3.5 days` datum remains `File_34`-controlled. `File_02` uses the whole-year `17980` and `24510` states for local arithmetic.

The cumulative Flood Week ranges, the Solar / Enochian split, the BJ / Jubilees `343` comparison, and the `EnvNorm` / `EnvSpread` distinction remain as approved in prior passes. No further author-decision blocker remains; the remaining dependency notes are retained as dependency controls rather than blockers.

### 0.10 Finalization Pass scope and result

Finalization Pass scope:
This pass marks the file Final, regularizes final-source status, confirms that prior passes have been incorporated, and preserves the remaining dependency controls in the audit ledger. It does not perform pressure testing, open new dependency-file derivations, recompute SKL/Berossus tables, alter arithmetic, or revise modal-state logic.

Finalization Pass result:
`File_02` is now Final. No author-decision issues remain open. LXX Long Count `3242 BC`, the SKL Short / Long appended `3 months and 3.5 days` datum, and SP/LXX 1656 endpoint relations remain dependency-controlled rather than unresolved blockers. Pressure testing was not part of the Finalization Pass; the post-final pressure-test result is recorded in §0.11 and §8.8.

### 0.11 Post-final Pressure Test scope and result

Pressure Test scope:
The post-final pressure test checked the final source for header/status consistency, one-H1 structure, table consistency, no-comma number formatting, modal-state register completeness, active-state restatement, operator firewalling, same-side and cross-axis arithmetic, Key-of-23 conversions, SKL rail arithmetic, Flood Week envelope handling, claim-status order, dependency boundaries, and Revision Log consistency.
```

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Files 36–40 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_40 Day 2 Correction

Title: Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — Files 36–40 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_40 Day 2 Correction

## Purpose

This addendum records the public-clean cleanup of `File_36` through `File_40` and the follow-up `File_40` v1.1 correction.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-finalized Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown source retained as the canonical layer.

The follow-up `File_40` v1.1 correction was author-directed after public-clean cleanup. It removes the erroneous Day 2 named-mechanism tag `Priestly expansion`, clears the associated audit flag, and preserves the only intended Day 2 point:

```markdown
20886 − 19416 = 1470 = 3 × 490 = 7 × 210
```

No arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, Machine Guards, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were changed. `File_40` Day 2 claim-status wording was corrected by author direction: the erroneous `source assertion; arithmetic unstated` / open-audit mechanism language was removed because the active claim is the arithmetic P19-to-SKL-Flood relation only.

Intermediate archive note:
An earlier intermediate Files 36–40 archive record preserved the File_40 Day 2 mechanism flag as open. This addendum supersedes that stale archive disposition. The active File_40 public-clean source is `File_40_Public_Clean_v1_1.md`, and the Day 2 issue is closed.

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — File_51a / File_54 / File_55 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_51a / File_55 Control Registration

File: Repository_Change_Archive_Addendum_File_51a_File_54_File_55_Public_Clean_Control_Registration
Title: Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — File_51a / File_54 / File_55 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_51a / File_55 Control Registration
Status: Historical record; non-controlling; produced June 27 2026 during public-clean publication cleanup and the follow-up bounded File_51a / File_55 control-registration update
Canonical source: Markdown
Control basis before follow-up: 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5; Restart Capsule v11.12; State Vocabulary Register v1.13; Project Procedures v3.2; File_00 public-clean archive-routing precedent; File_51a and File_54 style / retrieval precedents.
Control basis after follow-up: 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5; Restart Capsule v11.13; State Vocabulary Register v1.14; Project Procedures v3.2.

## Purpose

This addendum records two connected operations.

1. The public-clean cleanup of `File_51a`, `File_54`, and `File_55`.
2. The immediate follow-up bounded control registration that closed the `File_51a` handoff issue and the `File_55` standard SKL Creation rail-contraction registration issue.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-finalized Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown source retained as the canonical layer.

The follow-up control-registration pass was also not a substantive revision pass. It added state-vocabulary and Machine Guard retrieval controls for already-present local logic, advanced the Register to v1.14, advanced the Restart Capsule to v11.13, and refreshed the Style Guide v2.5 active companion-control pointer.

No arithmetic, anchors, active modal-state logic, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, claim-status labels, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were changed.

Raw `File_51b` was not required for this update. `File_51b` remains needed only when its own public-clean cleanup begins.

## File_51b revision sequence record

### Pass 0

Pass 0 completed the File Intake Sheet, risk rating, active-state inventory, primary-anchor inventory, major-operator inventory, dependency map, likely problem areas, section-level revision plan, audit notes, and controlling exemplars for raw `File_51b`.

No file text was revised during Pass 0.

### Pass 1

Pass 1 converted the inherited raw former `File_51a` §14 material into current repository form. It added:

- current file header and H1;
- Related Repository Files table;
- working state register;
- File map;
- Machine Guards;
- Smith LXX source-reconstruction note;
- claim-status restraint;
- audit notes;
- unresolved-issues section;
- recommended cross-file updates;
- Revision Log.

Pass 1 also normalized raw sectioning from former §14 into local `File_51b` numbering, converted tabular material to Markdown tables, and removed comma-grouped numerals such as `11111`, `11110`, `11100`, `22222`, `8000000`, and `4000000`.

No arithmetic, anchors, modal states, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, Mirror protocols, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were intentionally changed.

### Pass 2 and author-decision integration

Pass 2 verified the Pass 1 source, then integrated author decisions.

Author decisions applied:

| Issue | Final handling |
|---|---|
| LXX `5556 BC` | Resolved as actual LXX `+60 Terah` / Year-6 state, not a rounded state. The `5556 + 5556 − 1 = 11111` expression is retained as a completed Year-6 boundary expression, not a second civil-span operator. |
| `11111` | Resolved as a base-10 palindrome bridge. `File_51b` may state the local palindrome fact; `File_52a` / `File_52b` retain the inverse-number proof burden. |
| SP `4446 BC` local scaling | Pruned as non-load-bearing to avoid confusion. SP base-10 scaling section, state-register row, Machine Guard, unresolved issue, anchor-list entries, and recommended Register row removed. |
| Actual / rounded labels | Tightened through explicit node-class and state labels. |
| Proof-language calibration | Strong proof, design, irreducibility, and perfection language removed from the active argument. The file states arithmetic displays and dependency boundaries without making independent design claims. |
| Protocol 2 | Retained as midpoint-pivot logic. It is not a blanket `+1` correction to Protocol 1. |
| Biological / Levitical scaling | Clarified as dependency-controlled calendar / comparison material: `270 = 9 × 30`; `280 = 40 × 7`; male `7 + 33 = 40`; `280 + 40 = 320`; female `14 + 66 = 80`; `280 + 80 = 360`. |
| `§23.7` source reference | Resolved as a dependency reference to `File_51a`, §23.7, “The 5-Year Mod-5 Blocks.” |

### Source-reference resolution

The inherited `§23.7` reference in the Mod-5 Geometric Boundary Check was resolved as follows:

```markdown
Dependency note:
The inherited `§23.7` reference points to `File_51a`, §23.7, “The 5-Year Mod-5 Blocks.” That section controls the rounded mod-5 boundary-state block logic. `File_51b` may cite that boundary-state rule, but it does not replace `File_51a` as the controlling source for the mod-5 scaffold.
```

This closed the final unresolved source-reference issue before Finalization.

## Purpose

1. The public-clean cleanup of `File_41`, `File_42`, `File_43`, `File_44`, and `File_45`.
2. The author-directed bounded File_44 compact Register convention entry added after the Files 41–45 cleanup report recommended it.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-finalized Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown retained as the canonical source layer.

The File_44 Register follow-up was also not a substantive revision pass. It registered compact retrieval vocabulary and Machine Guards for state logic already present in `File_44`; it did not alter `File_44`, the Restart Capsule, the Style Guide, Project Procedures, or any repository arithmetic.

No arithmetic, anchors, active modal-state logic, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, Machine Guards, claim-status labels, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were changed.

## File_32 / File_48 mod-210 resolution record

### Scope

The author authorized the minimum safe fix for the File_48 mod-210 start-residue audit item.

The fix consisted of:

1. a compact File_32 clarification note under §1.6 Mod 210 corridor centers;
2. a File_48 audit-note replacement marking the item resolved.

This was a pointer-alignment and state-vocabulary closure only. It did not revise the prime-grid arithmetic, change the Modulo 210 corridor state, create a new civil-date operator, or change File_48's statistical claims.

### File_32 v1.1 compact clarification

File_32 now states:

```markdown
Start-residue projection note:
The active File_32 Modulo 210 corridor state is stated by quad centers: `15`, `105`, and `195 mod 210`. When the same corridor is viewed from the prime-quadruple start `Q`, the corresponding start residues are `11`, `101`, and `191 mod 210`, because `Center = Q + 4`.

This start-residue view is used by `File_48` for cumulative-prime gap accumulation. It is a projection of the same File_32 prime-lattice corridor state, not a redefinition of the Modulo 210 corridor and not a civil-date operator.
```

### File_48 v1.1 resolved note

File_48 now states:

```markdown
File_32 start-residue projection:
File_48's `11 / 101 / 191` mod-210 observation concerns prime-quadruple start residues used in cumulative-prime gap accumulation. File_32 states the same Modulo 210 corridor by quad centers `15 / 105 / 195`. Since `Center = Q + 4`, the two forms agree: `11 → 15`, `101 → 105`, and `191 → 195`.

This is convergence with File_32's Modulo 210 corridor state, not a new File_48 corridor definition and not a civil-date operator.

No recommended cross-file update remains from this issue.
```

### Disposition

|---|---|
| `File_32` | Updated to `File_32_Public_Clean_v1_1.md` with compact start-residue projection note. |

| State Vocabulary Register | No update required; v1.16 already recognizes the distinction between center corridors and start-residue projections. |
| Restart Capsule | No update required. |

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — File_50 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_50a Register Convention Entry

File: Repository_Change_Archive_Addendum_File_50_Public_Clean_File50a_Register_Convention
Title: Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — File_50 Public-Clean Cleanup and File_50a Register Convention Entry
Status: Historical record; non-controlling; produced June 27 2026 during public-clean publication cleanup for `File_50a_50b`, `File_50c`, and `File_50d_50e`, followed by the bounded File_50a compact Register convention entry update
Canonical source: Markdown
Archive basis: `Repository_Change_Archive_Files_01_49_51a_51b_54_55_File32_File48_Mod210_Update_Full_Replacement_v1_14.md` preserved exactly as prefix.
Control basis before File_50a Register follow-up: 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5; Restart Capsule v11.14; State Vocabulary Register v1.16; Project Procedures v3.2; File_00 public-clean archive-routing precedent.
Control basis after File_50a Register follow-up: 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5; Restart Capsule v11.15; State Vocabulary Register v1.17; Project Procedures v3.2.

## Purpose

This addendum records two connected operations.

1. The public-clean cleanup of the File_50 branch files supplied in this batch: `File_50a_50b`, `File_50c`, and `File_50d_50e`.
2. The author-directed bounded File_50a compact Register convention entry added after the File_50 cleanup report identified File_50a direct retrieval as more useful than a separate File_50b entry.

The cleanup was not a substantive revision pass. It prepared already-finalized or source-pointer Markdown files for one stable WordPress page per file on 490d.com, with raw Markdown retained as the canonical source layer.

The File_50a Register follow-up was also not a substantive revision pass. It registered compact retrieval vocabulary and Machine Guards for File_50a's already-present Naturalized Master Table and Base-90 grid mechanics. It did not change File_50a table arithmetic, File_50c executable landing mechanics, File_50d / File_50e summary logic, File_50b interpretive-synthesis status, or any dependency boundary.

No arithmetic, anchors, active modal-state logic, node-classes, sign conventions, operators, slash-pairs, ranges, envelopes, Mirror protocols, Machine Guards, claim-status labels, theological claims, or dependency boundaries were changed.

# Repository_Change_Archive Addendum — File_50a_50b / File_50c Post-Equalization Pressure Tests

Status: Historical record; non-controlling; appended after post-equalization sampled QA pressure tests of File_50a_50b and File_50c; active controls remain 490d Repository Style Guide v2.5, Restart Capsule v11.15, Project Procedures v3.2, and State_Vocabulary_Register v1.17.

## Summary

This addendum records the paired pressure tests of the File_50 Naturalized Grid and Bipartite Grid Landing layers after the repository-wide trust-status equalization pass.

| File | Result | Replacement |
|---|---|---|
| `File_50a_50b` | Passed pressure test; two non-argument metadata / trust-status corrections applied | `File_50a_50b_Public_Clean_v1_1_Trust_Status_Equalized_Pressure_Tested_v1_1.md` |
| `File_50c` | Passed pressure test; pressure-test status line updated to record current sampled QA pressure test | `File_50c_Public_Clean_v1_1_Trust_Status_Equalized_Pressure_Tested_v1_1.md` |

## File_50a_50b pressure-test note

The Naturalized Master Table was checked row by row against `P_nat = P − 11`, `V = round(P_nat / 90) × 90`, `Civil = V + 6`, and `D = P_nat − V`. All 100 rows passed. Every Original value was checked as a valid prime-quadruplet start. The P1 bypass, P2 grid-origin state, P2–P100 mod-30 / mod-210 residue rules, and verified Diff distribution `35 / 34 / 30` were retained. The File_50b P1–P24 subset was checked against the master table. No arithmetic or argument-bearing content changed.

Corrections applied: one stale audit-note sentence saying the 100-row table was not reproduced was corrected because §1.1a now carries the table; the top Revision basis was expanded to include `File_00 foundation / archive-routing precedent`.

## File_50c pressure-test note

The Bipartite Grid Landing operator, exact-strike inventory, Appendix A target matrices, target-admission rules, and hit ledger were checked against the File_50a master-table inputs. The scan reconfirmed `72` silent indices, `28` hitting indices, and `16` structural target families. All primary and source-parenthetical coordinate triples preserve exact `720` spacing; the Sumerian B / SKL Long rows preserve the stated `+6480` shift relative to Sumerian A / SKL Short through the Flood row. No target matrix, hit set, target count, arithmetic, or argument-bearing content changed.

## Control note

These pressure tests are historical / QA records. They do not alter active Style Guide, Restart Capsule, Project Procedures, State Vocabulary Register, or File_00 controls.
